2026-08-02 12:14:51 PDT, add deterministic automatic bank placement
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
* Complete detailed bankswitching roadmap item 7.  Before assigning final
  addresses, the linker now gathers every selected bank-owned private CODE and
  RODATA layout, pins `main`, startup/non-private runtime material, reserved-name
  private runtime functions, and explicitly named `mem` layouts, then collapses
  every cross-bank-forbidden ROM relocation and retained branch into an
  indivisible hard same-bank component.  Contradictory inherited or explicit
  pins fail before ordinary layout.
* Treat direct JSR and direct absolute JMP relationships as soft placement edges
  because the replicated trampoline table can implement them.  Use deterministic
  bridge-payload weights of 15 for JSR and 8 for JMP, reserve fixed initialized
  ROM images and generated copy/zero/init tables first, assign pins in stable
  order, then place unpinned components by decreasing size, decreasing soft-edge
  degree, and stable input order.  Choose the candidate bank with minimum
  incremental cut weight; ties prefer BANK0/startup, then the higher logical
  address and bank name.
* Preserve hard programmer control through existing named `mem` modifiers.  A
  `const` object defined in a named `$ro` region now emits an independently
  placeable `RODATA.region.__vcsc_object$NAME` layout.  Reject mutable definitions
  and runtime-only initializers in read-only named regions instead of silently
  creating DATA/BSS that startup would need to write.
* Add `BANK PLACEMENT` map reporting for component number, pinned/automatic
  assignment, logical bank, concrete MEMORY region, component bytes, incident
  cut weight, member layout, and input object.  Capacity diagnostics report the
  component size and free ordinary-ROM bytes in every candidate bank; final
  alignment, page, branch, and hole constraints remain enforced by the normal
  allocator.
* Add `test/linker_banked_auto_placement.pl`.  It locks deterministic repeated
  links, call-connected home placement, capacity spill, hard ROM-data affinity,
  contradictory pins, BANK0 `main`, expected bridge counts, and a source-compiled
  named `$ro` object whose unpinned reader follows it into BANK1.  Add three
  compile-only tests for read-only named object emission and rejection cases,
  and update compiler, linker, test, source-hygiene, and detailed bankswitching
  documentation.  Detailed roadmap item 8, a certified installed
  `vcs_8k_f8.cfg` profile and complete diagnostic cartridge, is next.
* Verification: all 344 compile-only tests pass.  All 208 end-to-end/generic
  tests pass; the full ordered run reached 165 before the command window, then
  the remaining 43 passed in deterministic groups of 27 and 16.  Focused F8/F6/
  F4 image, reset, relocation/trampoline, weighted-stack, automatic-placement,
  and source-hygiene tests pass.  Staged `make installcheck` passes using the
  supplied bison/flex packages.


2026-08-02 12:33:41 PDT, certify the installed F8 profile and record explicit bank duplication
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
* Complete detailed bankswitching roadmap item 8 by adding the public installed
  `libraries/vcs/vcs_8k_f8.cfg` profile.  It uses VCSC BANK1 at `$D000` as
  physical/file chunk 0 selected by `$1FF8`, VCSC BANK0 at `$F000` as the
  startup/home chunk 1 selected by `$1FF9`, and writes BANK0 last.  Ordinary
  allocation ends at `$xEFF`; `$xF00-$xFDF` is the byte-identical common
  trampoline corridor, `$xFE0-$xFF1` is the byte-identical vector bridge,
  `$xFF2-$xFF9` is reserved tail/hotspot space, and vectors occupy
  `$xFFA-$xFFF` in both banks.
* Name the public allocatable regions `bank0` and `bank1`, with matching
  `CODE.bankN` and `RODATA.bankN` segment rules, so existing named `mem`
  modifiers provide hard pins while unmarked private CODE/RODATA remains
  eligible for deterministic automatic placement.  Keep `main`, startup, and
  required runtime material in BANK0.
* Add the private `test/fixtures/bankswitching/f8_profile_diagnostic.c26` and
  `test/vcs_f8_profile.pl`.  The source diagnostic proves explicit BANK0/BANK1
  pins, an unpinned reader following a pinned BANK1 const object, cross-bank
  JMP, nested cross-bank JSR, byte-identical common corridors, exact selector
  identities, map output, and exact 8192-byte output.  A small profile-specific
  opcode model starts from both possible initially selected physical chunks,
  follows the reset bridge into BANK0, executes BANK0 -> BANK1 -> BANK0 nested
  calls, and proves LIFO bank restoration and balanced hardware-stack returns.
* Install and uninstall `share/vcs/vcs_8k_f8.cfg`; extend `make installcheck` to
  compile the diagnostic with the staged driver/profile and verify the exact
  image size and BANK0/BANK1 map identities.  Update the VCS catalog, driver,
  linker, test, source-hygiene, and generic-target-retention documentation.
* Add a new roadmap item immediately after automatic Superchip allocation for
  explicit multi-bank duplication.  Planned syntax such as
  `bank0 bank1 const uint8_t table[] := { ... };` creates one immutable object
  copy per listed bank and binds references to the local copy.  The same
  multi-region syntax will duplicate functions and prefer a local function copy
  before generating a cross-bank bridge.  Region sets are order-insensitive but
  must agree across declarations/definitions; duplicate region names and mutable
  object replication are errors.  Combining `inline` with any named bank or
  memory-region specification is always an error.
* Detailed bankswitching roadmap item 9, public F6 and F4 profiles through the
  same implementation, is next.

2026-08-02 13:46:34 PDT, certify public F6/F4 profiles and lock Superchip allocation spelling
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
* Complete detailed bankswitching roadmap item 9 by adding the public installed
  `libraries/vcs/vcs_16k_f6.cfg` and `libraries/vcs/vcs_32k_f4.cfg` profiles
  through the existing full-window implementation.  F6 writes VCSC
  BANK3/BANK2/BANK1/BANK0 in physical-file order with `$1FF6-$1FF9`; F4 writes
  BANK7 through BANK0 with `$1FF4-$1FFB`.  BANK0 remains the `$F000` home bank
  and final file chunk in both profiles.
* Reserve the same `$xF00-$xFDF` trampoline table, `$xFE0-$xFF1` vector bridge,
  `$xFF2-$xFF9` tail, and `$xFFA-$xFFF` vectors in every physical bank.  F4's
  `$1FFA/$1FFB` NMI-vector overlap remains deterministic because every physical
  bank contains identical vector bytes and the fetch finishes with BANK0
  selected.
* Add `test/vcs_f6_f4_profiles.pl`.  It assembles a nested call chain with one
  function in every logical bank, starts from every possible initially selected
  physical chunk, exercises every mapper selector on outward and return paths,
  verifies LIFO bank restoration and balanced hardware stack, locks exact
  logical-bank/file-index/hotspot map identities, byte-identical common
  corridors, reserved hotspot bytes, F4 vector overlap, and exact 16K/32K image
  sizes.
* Install and uninstall both profiles, extend staged `make installcheck` to
  compile diagnostics through F8, F6, and F4, and update the VCS catalog,
  driver/linker/test documentation, generic-target retention, and source-tree
  hygiene checks.
* Lock the planned automatic Superchip allocation spelling and address order in
  `.top_secret/bankswitching.txt`: an SC header will define
  `mem superchip { $read_start:0xF080 $write_start:0xF000 $size:0x80 $rw };`,
  applications will write `superchip uint8_t foo;`, and the explicit equivalent
  is `ref uint8_t foo@[0xF080/0xF000];`.  Existing syntax is always
  `@[read_address/write_address]`; loads use the read alias and stores,
  initializers, and BSS clearing use the write alias.  The 128 bytes are one
  shared physical region across every bank.

  tests pass; the ordered run reached 165 before the command window and the
  remaining 45 passed in two groups of 22 plus the one legacy legal-byte test.
  Staged `make installcheck` passes with the supplied bison/flex packages.
* Detailed bankswitching roadmap item 10, fully bank-aware archive/listing/map
  and simulator execution, is next.
